# Second-Round Reviewer Empirical Analyses

This report is generated by `scripts/run_second_round_reviewer_analyses.py`.
It uses existing synthetic and real subject-verb caches only; no new GPU run is required.

## Conclusions

| gap                            | status               | headline_metric                                                     | conclusion                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | table                                     |
|:-------------------------------|:---------------------|:--------------------------------------------------------------------|:---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|:------------------------------------------|
| BH/BY decoy coverage           | empirical_conclusion | mean decoys selected: e-BH=0.001, BY=0.111, BH=1.233                | BH/BY are now reported for every layer and decoy scheme. They often remain cleaner than uncorrected p<q, but can admit more constructed-null decoys than e-BH under screen-attractive decoys.                                                                              | table_18_second_round_decoy_bh_by         |
| Gamma sensitivity              | empirical_conclusion | real nondefault gamma median Jaccard=0.611; synthetic max FDP=0.004 | Gamma changes discovery counts but the nondefault real selections retain measurable overlap with gamma=0.5. Synthetic FDP stays below q across the requested gamma grid; gamma=0.5 remains the balanced default between conservative gamma=0.25 and aggressive gamma=0.75. | tables_19_20_second_round_gamma           |
| Real CI width distribution     | empirical_conclusion | e-BH selected CI width median=0.0266; p90=0.1398                    | Real selected features now have a per-feature uncertainty distribution rather than only point discoveries; widths vary by layer and selection set and are visualized directly.                                                                                             | table_21_second_round_real_ci_type_s      |
| Real Type S diagnostic         | empirical_conclusion | e-BH diagnostic rate median=0.000; max sign-flip rate=0.000         | Real Type S cannot be verified against unknown ground truth, so the paper should describe this as a sign-instability diagnostic: CI zero-crossings plus cluster-bootstrap sign flips. The selected e-BH features have low diagnostic rates.                                | table_21_second_round_real_ci_type_s      |
| Covariate-adaptive e-BH        | empirical_conclusion | synthetic stratified FDP=0.001; real median discovery gain=0.0      | The screen-stratified e-BH variant preserves an arbitrary-dependence guarantee by splitting q across disjoint screen-score strata. It is a valid comparator, but its power gain is empirical and not guaranteed; report gains and losses layer by layer.                   | table_22_second_round_adaptive_ebh        |
| Cluster permutation robustness | empirical_conclusion | cluster conservative e-BH median Jaccard=0.840                      | Cluster-level tests address prompt/patch dependence by reducing the test unit to prompt clusters. Main discoveries remain partially stable under cluster-mean and cluster-signflip normal tests; finite randomization remains resolution-limited for p-to-e e-BH.          | table_23_second_round_cluster_permutation |

## second_round_conclusions.csv

| gap                            | status               | headline_metric                                                     | conclusion                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | table                                     |
|:-------------------------------|:---------------------|:--------------------------------------------------------------------|:---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|:------------------------------------------|
| BH/BY decoy coverage           | empirical_conclusion | mean decoys selected: e-BH=0.001, BY=0.111, BH=1.233                | BH/BY are now reported for every layer and decoy scheme. They often remain cleaner than uncorrected p<q, but can admit more constructed-null decoys than e-BH under screen-attractive decoys.                                                                              | table_18_second_round_decoy_bh_by         |
| Gamma sensitivity              | empirical_conclusion | real nondefault gamma median Jaccard=0.611; synthetic max FDP=0.004 | Gamma changes discovery counts but the nondefault real selections retain measurable overlap with gamma=0.5. Synthetic FDP stays below q across the requested gamma grid; gamma=0.5 remains the balanced default between conservative gamma=0.25 and aggressive gamma=0.75. | tables_19_20_second_round_gamma           |
| Real CI width distribution     | empirical_conclusion | e-BH selected CI width median=0.0266; p90=0.1398                    | Real selected features now have a per-feature uncertainty distribution rather than only point discoveries; widths vary by layer and selection set and are visualized directly.                                                                                             | table_21_second_round_real_ci_type_s      |
| Real Type S diagnostic         | empirical_conclusion | e-BH diagnostic rate median=0.000; max sign-flip rate=0.000         | Real Type S cannot be verified against unknown ground truth, so the paper should describe this as a sign-instability diagnostic: CI zero-crossings plus cluster-bootstrap sign flips. The selected e-BH features have low diagnostic rates.                                | table_21_second_round_real_ci_type_s      |
| Covariate-adaptive e-BH        | empirical_conclusion | synthetic stratified FDP=0.001; real median discovery gain=0.0      | The screen-stratified e-BH variant preserves an arbitrary-dependence guarantee by splitting q across disjoint screen-score strata. It is a valid comparator, but its power gain is empirical and not guaranteed; report gains and losses layer by layer.                   | table_22_second_round_adaptive_ebh        |
| Cluster permutation robustness | empirical_conclusion | cluster conservative e-BH median Jaccard=0.840                      | Cluster-level tests address prompt/patch dependence by reducing the test unit to prompt clusters. Main discoveries remain partially stable under cluster-mean and cluster-signflip normal tests; finite randomization remains resolution-limited for p-to-e e-BH.          | table_23_second_round_cluster_permutation |
